````math
Lemma [F.3](#A6.Thmtheorem3 "Lemma F.3. ‣ F.3.1 Step 1: decoupling statistical dependency ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
for $`N_{h}(s,\bm{a})>0`$ and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,\bm{a}}\right)}\!P\widehat{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}-\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}\big(\widehat{P}_{h,s,\bm{a}}^{0}\big)}P\widehat{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}\right|\leq H=\beta_{i,h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)\text{ for }N_{h}(s,\bm{a})=0;`$ |  | (162) |

and (ii) is valid under the notation

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h,s,\bm{a}}\coloneqq\mathrm{argmin}_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big(P^{0}_{h,s,\bm{a}}\big)}{P}\underline{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}`$ |  | (163) |

and consequently,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,\bm{a}}\right)}P\underline{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}={P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h,s,\bm{a}}\underline{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}},\mbox{ and }\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,\bm{a}}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}\leq{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h,s,\bm{a}}\widehat{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}.`$ |  |

For ease of proof, we introduce a notation as
$`\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h,s}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\pi_{i}(s),{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))}{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h,s,\bm{a}}`$.
Furthermore, we define a sequence of matrices
$`\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h}\in\mathbb{R}^{S\times S}`$. We can utilizing
([161](#A6.E161 "In F.3.2 Step 2: decomposing the error Gap(𝜋̂) ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
recursively over the time steps $`h,h+1,\cdots,H`$ and derive

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{i,h}^{\star,\widehat{\pi}_{-i},\sigma^{+}}(s)-V_{i,h}^{\widehat{\pi},\sigma^{+}}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\widehat{V}_{i,h}^{\sigma_{i}}(s)-\underline{V}_{i,h}^{\sigma_{i}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h}\left(\widehat{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}-\underline{V}_{i,h+1}^{\sigma_{i}}\right)+3\beta_{i,h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h}\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h+1}\left(\widehat{V}_{i,h+2}^{\sigma_{i}}-\underline{V}_{i,h+2}^{\sigma_{i}}\right)+3\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,h}\beta_{i,h+1}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}+3\beta_{i,h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\cdots\leq 3\sum_{i^{\prime}=h}^{H}\left(\prod_{j=h+1}^{i^{\prime}-1}\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,j}\right)\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}+3\beta_{i,h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=3\sum_{i^{\prime}=h}^{H}\left(\prod_{j=h}^{i^{\prime}-1}\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,j}\right)\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},`$ |  | (164) |

where we define
$`\left(\prod_{j=h}^{i^{\prime}-1}\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,j}\right)=I`$
for conciseness.

For any
$`d_{h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\in\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{pi}}_{h}`$
(cf. ([53](#A4.E53 "In 2nd item ‣ D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))),
taking inner product with
([59](#A4.Ex33 "In D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left<d_{h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},V_{i,h}^{\star,\widehat{\pi}_{-i},\sigma^{+}}-V_{i,h}^{\widehat{\pi},\sigma^{+}}\right>`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\left<d_{h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},3\sum_{i^{\prime}=h}^{H}\left(\prod_{j=h}^{i^{\prime}-1}\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,j}\right)\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\right>`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=3\sum_{i^{\prime}=h}^{H}\left\langle d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\right\rangle,`$ |  | (165) |

where

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i}^{\sf{d}},\pi_{-i}^{\star}}\coloneqq\left[\big(d_{h}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\big)^{\top}\left(\prod_{j=h}^{i^{\prime}-1}\check{P}^{\inf,V}_{i,j}\right)\right]^{\top}\in\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{pi}}_{i^{\prime}}`$ |  | (166) |

by the definition of $`\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{pi}}_{i^{\prime}}`$
(cf. ([159](#A6.E159 "In 2nd item ‣ F.3.2 Step 2: decomposing the error Gap(𝜋̂) ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")))
for all $`{i^{\prime}}=h+1,\cdots,H`$.

Next, we control
$`\langle d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\rangle`$
utilizing concentrability. First of all, according to the definition of
penalty, we demonstrate that the pessimistic penalty satisfies

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a},\hat{V})`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathsf{n}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{i,s,\bm{a}}}(\widehat{V})},\frac{2C_{\mathsf{n}}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathsf{n}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{i,s,\bm{a}}}(\widehat{V})}+\frac{2C_{\mathsf{n}}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathsf{n}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}\left(2\mathsf{Var}_{P^{0}_{i,s,\bm{a}}}\big({\widehat{V}}\big)+\frac{C_{0}H^{2}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}\right)}+\frac{2C_{\mathsf{n}}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}\sqrt{\frac{2C_{\mathsf{n}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{P^{0}_{i,s,\bm{a}}}\big({\widehat{V}}\big)}+\frac{\left(2C_{\mathsf{n}}+\sqrt{C_{\mathsf{n}}C_{0}}\right)H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}`$ |  | (167) |

where (i) holds by applying
([153](#A6.E153 "In Lemma F.3. ‣ F.3.1 Step 1: decoupling statistical dependency ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
for some sufficiently large $`C_{0}`$ and (ii) exists follows from the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Therefore, combining the definition of
$`\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)`$, we obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\langle d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\rangle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\pi_{i}(s),{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))}\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a},\hat{V})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,\bm{a})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s)\ind\{a_{i}=\pi_{i}^{\star}(s)\}{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{d}}(\mathbf{a}_{-i}|s)\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a},\hat{V})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,{a}_{i})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}(s,\pi_{i}^{\sf{d}}(s),\mathbf{a}_{-i},\hat{V}),`$ |  | (168) |

where the last equation holds due to the definition in
([142b](#A6.E142.2 "In 142 ‣ F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
Then, we observe
$`d_{h}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,\bm{a})\in\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{pai}}_{h}`$
(cf. ([160](#A6.E160 "In 2nd item ‣ F.3.2 Step 2: decomposing the error Gap(𝜋̂) ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))).
Thereafter, we divide the bound
([168](#A6.Ex25 "In F.3.2 Step 2: decomposing the error Gap(𝜋̂) ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
into two cases.

For the first case, i.e., $`s\in S`$ where
$`\max_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}(P^{0})}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}},P}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)=0`$,
it follows from the definition
(cf. ([159](#A6.E159 "In 2nd item ‣ F.3.2 Step 2: decomposing the error Gap(𝜋̂) ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")))
that for any
$`d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))\in\mathcal{D}^{\mathsf{pai}}_{i}`$,
it satisfies that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))=0.`$ |  | (169) |

For the second case, i.e., $`s\in S`$ where
$`\max_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{0})}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}},P}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)>0`$,
by the assumption
in ([151](#A6.E151 "In Assumption F.2 (Robust multiple clipped concentrability). ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}(P^{0})}\frac{\min\big\{d_{i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}},P}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big),\frac{1}{S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}}\big\}}{d^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)}\leq C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}<\infty.`$ |  |

It implies that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)>0\quad\text{and}\quad\big(i^{\prime},s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)\in\mathcal{C}^{\mathsf{n}}.`$ |  | (170) |

Lemma [F.1](#A6.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma F.1. ‣ F.2 Multi-RTZ-VI-LCB ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
tells that with probability at least $`1-8\delta`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle N_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\frac{Kd^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)}{8}-5\sqrt{Kd^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\geq}\frac{Kd^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)}{16}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\geq}\frac{K\max_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma_{i}}(P^{0})}\min\left\{d_{i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}},P}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big),\frac{1}{S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}}\right\}}{16C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\frac{K\min\left\{d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)),\frac{1}{S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}}\right\}}{16C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}},`$ |  | (171) |

where (ii) comes from
Assumption [F.2](#A6.Thmtheorem2 "Assumption F.2 (Robust multiple clipped concentrability). ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
and (i) holds due to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Kd^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq c_{0}\frac{HS\sum_{i=1}A_{i}}{d_{\mathsf{m}}^{\mathsf{n}}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}f(\{\sigma_{i}\}_{i=1}^{m},H)d^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq c_{0}HS\sum_{i=1}A_{i}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}f(\{\sigma_{i}\}_{i=1}^{m},H)\geq 1600\log\frac{KH}{\delta},`$ |  | (172) |

where
$`f(\{\sigma_{i}\}_{i=1}^{m},H)=\min\left\{\left\{\frac{(H\sigma_{i}-1+(1-\sigma_{i})^{H})}{(\sigma_{i})^{2}}\right\}_{i=1}^{m},H\right\}`$,
the first inequality follows from condition
([26](#S4.E26 "In Theorem 4.5 (Upper bound for Multi-RTZ-VI-LCB). ‣ Robust unilateral clipped concentrability. ‣ 4 Performance Guarantees ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
and the second inequality follows from

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
d_{\mathsf{m}}^{\mathsf{n}}=\min_{h,s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)}\left\{d^{\mathsf{n}}_{h}(s,\pi_{i}^{\sf{d}}(s),\mathbf{a}_{-i}):d^{\mathsf{n}}_{h}(s,\pi_{i}^{\sf{d}}(s),\mathbf{a}_{-i})>0\right\}\leq d^{\mathsf{n}}_{i^{\prime}}\big(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\big).
``` |  | (173) |

Combining the results in
([63](#A4.Ex36 "In D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([64](#A4.Ex39 "In D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we arrive at

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\langle d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}},\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}^{\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}\rangle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,{a}_{i})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))\beta_{i,i^{\prime}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s),\hat{V})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,{a}_{i})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))\sqrt{\frac{2C_{\mathsf{n}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{P^{0}_{i,s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)}}\big({\widehat{V}}\big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{(s,{a}_{i})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))\frac{\left(2C_{\mathsf{n}}+\sqrt{C_{\mathsf{n}}C_{0}}\right)H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{i}\left(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,{a}_{i})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}}d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s))\Bigg(\frac{16C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}\left(2C_{\mathsf{n}}+\sqrt{C_{\mathsf{n}}C_{0}}\right)H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{K\min\left\{d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)),\frac{1}{S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}}\right\}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{32C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}C_{\mathsf{n}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{K\min\left\{d_{i^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{p},\pi_{i},{\pi}_{-i}^{\sf{b}}}(s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)),\frac{1}{S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}}\right\}}\mathsf{Var}_{P^{0}_{i,s,a_{i},\pi_{-i}^{\sf{b}}(s)}}\big({\widehat{V}}\big)}\Bigg).`$ |  | (174) |

Similar to the proof in
Appendix [D.2](#A4.SS2 "D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"),
we are ready to bound
$`V_{i,h}^{\star,\widehat{\pi}_{-i},\sigma^{+}}(\varrho)-V_{i,h}^{\widehat{\pi},\sigma^{+}}(\varrho)`$.
There exists some sufficiently large constants $`C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}>0`$,
and

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{i,h}^{\star,\widehat{\pi}_{-i},\sigma^{+}}(\varrho)-V_{i,h}^{\widehat{\pi},\sigma^{+}}(\varrho)\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}C_{1}H^{3}S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{K}\min\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma_{i}-1+(1-\sigma_{i})^{H})}{(\sigma_{i})^{2}},H\right\}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}C_{2}H^{2}S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{K}\min\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma_{i}-1+(1-\sigma_{i})^{H})}{(\sigma_{i})^{2}},H\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}C_{3}H^{3}S\sum_{i=1}A_{i}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{K}{\min\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma_{i}-1+(1-\sigma_{i})^{H})}{(\sigma_{i})^{2}},H\right\}}},`$ |  | (175) |

where the last inequality follows from condition
([26](#S4.E26 "In Theorem 4.5 (Upper bound for Multi-RTZ-VI-LCB). ‣ Robust unilateral clipped concentrability. ‣ 4 Performance Guarantees ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

#### F.3.3 Step 3: summing up the results

Consequently, we obtain the upper bound of
$`V_{i,1}^{\star,\widehat{\pi}_{-i},\sigma_{i}}(\varrho)-V_{i,1}^{\widehat{\pi},\sigma_{i}}(\varrho)`$
in
([175](#A6.Ex37 "In F.3.2 Step 2: decomposing the error Gap(𝜋̂) ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
which directly leads to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathrm{Gap}(\widehat{\pi})\leq c_{1}\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathsf{r}}^{\star}H^{2}S\sum_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{K}\min\left\{\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma_{i}-1+(1-\sigma_{i})^{H})}{(\sigma_{i})^{2}}\right\}_{i=1}^{m},H\right\}},`$ |  | (176) |

for some sufficiently large $`c_{1}`$ and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
K\geq HS\sum_{i=1}A_{i}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}\min\left\{\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma_{i}-1+(1-\sigma_{i})^{H})}{(\sigma_{i})^{2}}\right\}_{i=1}^{m},H\right\}.
``` |  |

## Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem [4.2](#S4.Thmtheorem2 "Theorem 4.2 (Upper bound for RTZ-VI-LCB). ‣ Robust unilateral clipped concentrability. ‣ 4 Performance Guarantees ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

### G.1 Proof of Lemma [D.1](#A4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

We prove
Lemma [D.1](#A4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
similar to the proof of Claim 1 introduced by \[[43](#bib.bib41)\].

#### G.1.1 Proof of ([46](#A4.E46 "In Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

According to the definition in
([16](#S3.E16 "In Dual problem. ‣ 3.2 Optimistic Variant of Robust Value Iteration with Lower Confidence Bounds ‣ 3 Algorithm Design ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
for any fixed value vector $`V`$ independent of
$`\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}{P}V-\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}{P}V\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\Big|\max_{\alpha\in[\min_{s}V(s),\max_{s}V(s)]}\left\{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}-\sigma^{+}~\left(\alpha-\min_{s^{\prime}}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}(s^{\prime})\right)\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\max_{\alpha\in[\min_{s}V(s),\max_{s}V(s)]}\left\{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}-\sigma^{+}~\left(\alpha-\min_{s^{\prime}}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}(s^{\prime})\right)\right\}\Big|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{\alpha\in[\min_{s}V(s),\max_{s}V(s)]}\left|\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{\alpha\in[0,H]}\left|\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left[V\right]_{\alpha}\right|,`$ |  | (177) |

where the last inequality exists due to the fact that the maximum
operator is $`1`$-Lipschitz. According to the definition of empirical
transition kernel $`\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)\left[V\right]_{\alpha}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\underbrace{\left[V\left(s^{\prime}\right)\right]_{\alpha}\left[\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\ind\left\{h_{i}=h,s_{i}=s,a_{i}=a,b_{i}=b,s_{i}^{\prime}=s^{\prime}\right\}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}-P_{h}^{0}\left(s^{\prime}\,|\,s,a,b\right)\right]}_{\eqqcolon X_{s^{\prime}}}`$ |  |

as a sum of independent random variables. Based on the relationship
between $`{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}`$ and $`\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}`$, we
verify $`\mathbb{E}[X_{s^{\prime}}]=0`$ and $`|X_{s^{\prime}}|\leq H`$
for all $`s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}`$. With probability exceeding
$`1-\delta`$ and for some universal constant $`C_{4}>0`$, under the
Bernstein inequality \[[37](#bib.bib36), Theorem 2.8.4\], we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)\left[V\right]_{\alpha}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{P^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(\left[V\right]_{\alpha}\right)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}+\frac{C_{4}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{P^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}+\frac{C_{4}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)},`$ |  | (178) |

where the last inequality comes from the definition of
$`\left[V\right]_{\alpha}`$ in
([17](#S3.E17 "In Dual problem. ‣ 3.2 Optimistic Variant of Robust Value Iteration with Lower Confidence Bounds ‣ 3 Algorithm Design ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

Let $`\overline{V}\coloneqq V-\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)1`$, we
have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle={P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)+\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+\big[\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big]^{2}+\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big),`$ |  | (179) |

where the last equation holds since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left(\big[V-\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)1\big]\circ\big[V-\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)1\big]\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left(V\circ V\right)-2\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)+\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)^{2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\left(\big[V-\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)1\big]\circ\big[V-\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)1\big]\right)+\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)^{2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad-2\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)+\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}V\right)^{2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+\big[\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big]^{2}.`$ |  |

Analogous to
([178](#A7.E178 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
with probability exceeding $`1-\delta`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)\big|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{4}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}+\frac{C_{4}H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{4}\sqrt{\frac{H^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\big(V\big)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}+\frac{C_{4}H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)},`$ |  | (180) |

where the last inequation comes from the fact that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)\leq H^{2}{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)=H^{2}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right).`$ |  |

Employing
([180](#A7.Ex54 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we further bound
([179](#A7.E179 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+\big[\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big]^{2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+C_{4}\sqrt{\frac{H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\big(V\big)}+\frac{C_{4}H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+\big[\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big]^{2}+\frac{C_{4}H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+\frac{C_{4}^{2}H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{2N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)},`$ |  |

where the last relation holds due to the AM-GM inequality. Therefore, we
obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\leq 2\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+2\big[\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big]^{2}+\frac{\left(C_{4}^{2}+2C_{4}\right)H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}.
``` |  | (181) |

Combining
([181](#A7.E181 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([178](#A7.E178 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we derive

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2C_{4}^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}+\frac{\sqrt{C_{4}^{2}\left(C_{4}^{2}+2C_{4}\right)H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{2C_{4}^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}\,\big|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big|+\frac{C_{4}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}.`$ |  | (182) |

Next, we consider two cases, i.e.,
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)\leq\frac{1}{8C_{4}^{2}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$
and
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)>\frac{1}{8C_{4}^{2}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$.
In the first case of
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)\leq\frac{1}{8C_{4}^{2}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$,
([46](#A4.E46 "In Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
is valid since

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}{P}V-\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}{P}V\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\max_{\alpha\in[\min_{s}V(s),\max_{s}V(s)]}\left|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2H=O\bigg(\frac{H\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\bigg).`$ |  | (183) |

In the second case of
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)>\frac{1}{8C_{4}^{2}}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$,
it follows from
([182](#A7.E182 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\big|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big|+\sqrt{\frac{2C_{4}^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C_{4}+\sqrt{C_{4}^{2}\left(C_{4}^{2}+2C_{4}\right)}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}H\log\frac{KH}{\delta},`$ |  |

which, by arranging the terms, leads to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big|\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{8C_{4}^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2H\frac{C_{4}+\sqrt{C_{4}^{2}\left(C_{4}^{2}+2C_{4}\right)}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}.`$ |  | (184) |

Combining
([177](#A7.E177 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([184](#A7.E184 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}{P}V-\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}{P}V\right|\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{8C_{4}^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2H\frac{C_{4}+\sqrt{C_{4}^{2}\left(C_{4}^{2}+2C_{4}\right)}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}.`$ |  | (185) |

Joining these two cases, we conclude the proof of
([46](#A4.E46 "In Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

#### G.1.2 Proof of ([47](#A4.E47 "In Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

We consider two cases, i.e.,
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)<16C_{4}^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$ and
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)\geq 16C_{4}^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$. In the
first case of
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)<16C_{4}^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$,
([47](#A4.E47 "In Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
is valid since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)\leq H^{2}=O\left(\frac{H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\right).
``` |  |

In the second case of
$`N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)\geq 16C_{4}^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}`$, we
have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(i)}}{=}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)-\big[\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)V\big]^{2}-\big({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\big)\big(\overline{V}\circ\overline{V}\big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(ii)}}{\leq}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+C_{4}\sqrt{\frac{H^{2}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\big(V\big)\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}+\frac{C_{4}H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(iii)}}{\leq}2\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+\frac{\left(C_{4}^{2}/4+C_{4}\right)H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=2\mathsf{Var}_{{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}}\left(V\right)+O\left(\frac{H^{2}\log\frac{KH}{\delta}}{N_{h}\left(s,a,b\right)}\right),`$ |  |

where (i) comes from
([179](#A7.E179 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
(ii) holds due to
([180](#A7.Ex54 "In G.1.1 Proof of (). ‣ G.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
and (iii) is based on the AM-GM inequality.

Combining the two cases,
([47](#A4.E47 "In Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
holds and
Lemma [D.1](#A4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
is proved.

### G.2 Proof of Lemma [D.2](#A4.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma D.2. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

Assuming that
$`\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)\geq{Q}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s,a,b)`$,
then we can obtain
$`\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)\geq{V}_{h}^{\star,{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s)`$, since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}\left(s\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\mu_{h}^{+}\left(s\right),b\sim\nu_{h}^{+}(s)}\left[\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}\left(s,a,b\right)\right]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(i)}}{\geq}\mathbb{E}_{a\sim{\mu}^{\star}\left(s\right),b\sim\widehat{\nu}(s)}\left[\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}\left(s,a,b\right)\right]\geq\mathbb{E}_{a\sim{\mu}^{\star}\left(s\right),b\sim\widehat{\nu}(s)}\left[{Q}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}\left(s,a,b\right)\right]={V}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s),`$ |  |

where (i) holds due to the fact that $`\widehat{\nu}=\nu_{h}^{+}`$ and
$`(\mu_{h}^{+},\nu_{h}^{+})`$ is the Nash equilibrium of
$`\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}\left(s,a,b\right)`$. Hence, we need to verify

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)\geq{Q}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s,a,b),`$ |  | (186) |

which can be proved by mathematical induction. Specifically,
([186](#A7.E186 "In G.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
holds when $`h=H+1`$ under the trivial fact
$`\widehat{Q}_{H+1}^{+}(s,a,b)={Q}_{H+1}^{\star,\sigma^{+}}(s,a,b)=0.`$
Suppose that
([186](#A7.E186 "In G.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
holds for all
$`(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}`$ at some time
step $`h\in[H]`$. According to the update rule in line 4 in
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ Policy estimation. ‣ 3.2 Optimistic Variant of Robust Value Iteration with Lower Confidence Bounds ‣ 3 Algorithm Design ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"),
([186](#A7.E186 "In G.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
exists if $`\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)=H`$ because
$`\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)=H\geq{Q}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s,a,b)`$.
Besides, in the case of $`N_{h}(s,a,b)=0`$, we have
$`\beta_{h}\left(s,a,b,\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}\right)=H`$, leading to
$`\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)=H\geq{Q}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s,a,b)`$.
Otherwise, for $`N_{h}(s,a,b)>0`$, $`\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)`$ is
updated as

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}\left(s,a,b\right)=`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}\left(s,a,b\right)+\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+\beta_{h}\left(s,a,b,\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}\left(s,a,b\right)+\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+\beta_{h}\left(s,a,b,\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\left|\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}-\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}\left(s,a,b\right)+\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+0`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}\left(s,a,b\right)+\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left({P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right)}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}+0={Q}_{h}^{\star,\widehat{\nu},\sigma^{+}}(s,a,b),`$ |  | (187) |

where the second inequality holds due to
([48](#A4.E48 "In D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
in
Lemma [D.1](#A4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
and the last equality comes from the empirical robust Bellman equation
([30](#A2.E30 "In B.2 Facts of RTZMGs and empirical RTZMGs ‣ Appendix B Preliminaries ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
Together with the case of $`h=H+1`$, we complete prove
Lemma [D.2](#A4.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma D.2. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning").

### G.3 Proof of Lemma [D.3](#A4.Thmtheorem3 "Lemma D.3. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

Following the proof by Lemma 3 in \[[34](#bib.bib31)\], we bound
$`\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)`$ and
$`\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)`$. Specifically,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim{\mu}^{+}_{h}\times{\nu}^{+}_{h}}\left[\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim{\mu}^{+}_{h}\times{\nu}^{+}_{h}}\left[\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a,b)+\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}_{h,s,a,b}^{0})}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+\beta_{h}\left(s,a,b,\widehat{V}_{h+1}\right)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq 0+\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)+0,`$ |  | (188) |

where the second equality is valid due to the update rule in line 4 in
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ Policy estimation. ‣ 3.2 Optimistic Variant of Robust Value Iteration with Lower Confidence Bounds ‣ 3 Algorithm Design ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning").
Similarly,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim{\mu}^{+}_{h}\times{\nu}^{+}_{h}}\left[\widehat{Q}_{h}^{+}(s,a,b)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim{\mu}^{+}_{h}\times{\nu}^{+}_{h}}\left[\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a,b)+\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}_{h,s,a,b}^{0})}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+\beta_{h}\left(s,a,b,\widehat{V}_{h+1}\right)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 1+\max_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}_{h,s,a,b})}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+H.`$ |  | (189) |

For any $`h\in[H]`$, there exists at least one state $`s_{h}^{\star}`$
that satisfies
$`\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s_{h}^{\star})=\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)`$.
Furthermore, for any accessible uncertainty set $`\sigma^{+}>0`$ and
$`(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}`$, we define
an auxiliary vector $`\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}`$
by reducing the values of several elements of
$`\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}`$ strictly, namely,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
0\leq\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}\leq\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}(s^{\prime})-\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}(s^{\prime})=\left\|\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right\|_{1}=\sigma^{+}.
``` |  | (190) |

Let $`l_{s_{h}^{\star}}`$ represent an $`S`$-dimensional standard basis
under $`s_{h}^{\star}`$. We can derive that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}+\sigma^{+}\big[l_{s_{h}^{\star}}\big]^{\top}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right\|_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}-\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b}\right\|_{1}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\sigma^{+}\big[l_{s_{h}^{\star}}\big]^{\top}\right\|_{1}\leq\sigma^{+},`$ |  | (191) |

where the first inequality is valid since the ‘distance’ function (e.g.,
TV distance) satisfies the triangle inequality.

Therefore, we can conclude that
$`\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}+\sigma^{+}\big[l_{s_{h}^{\star}}\big]^{\top}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}^{0}_{h,s,a,b})`$
and
$`\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}+\sigma^{+}\big[l_{s_{h}^{\star}}\big]^{\top}`$
is a distribution vector based on
([191](#A7.E191 "In G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
leading to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(\widehat{P}_{h,s,a,b}^{0})}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\left(\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}+\sigma^{+}\big[l_{s_{i,h}^{\star}}\big]^{\top}\right)\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widehat{P}^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}\big\|_{1}\big\|\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}\big\|_{\infty}+\sigma^{+}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1}^{\star})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\sigma^{+}\right)\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)+\sigma^{+}\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s),`$ |  | (192) |

where the last inequality holds since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big\|P^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}\big\|_{1}=\sum_{s^{\prime}}P^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}(s^{\prime})=-\sum_{s^{\prime}}\left(P^{0}_{h,s,a,b}(s^{\prime})-P^{\prime}_{h,s,a,b}(s^{\prime})\right)+\sum_{s^{\prime}}P^{0}_{h,s,a,b}(s^{\prime})=1-\sigma^{+}.`$ |  | (193) |

Putting
([192](#A7.Ex81 "In G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([189](#A7.Ex79 "In G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
together shows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 1+\max_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{0}_{h,s,a,b})}P\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}+H`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq H+1+\left(1-\sigma^{+}\right)\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)+\sigma^{+}\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s).`$ |  | (194) |

By substituting
([194](#A7.Ex83 "In G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
it
([188](#A7.Ex77 "In G.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
it readily follows that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}-\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle H+1+\left(1-\sigma^{+}\right)\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)+\sigma^{+}\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)-\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}V_{h+1}^{+}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle H+1+(1-\sigma^{+})\left(\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)-\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+1}^{+}(s)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle H+1+(1-\sigma^{+})\left[H+1+(1-\sigma^{+})\left(\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+2}^{+}(s)-\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h+2}^{+}(s)\right)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\cdots\leq\frac{\left(H+1\right)\left(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H-h}\right)}{\sigma^{+}}.`$ |  | (195) |

which, combined with
$`\max_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)-\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{h}^{+}(s)\leq H`$,
conclude this proof.

### G.4 Proof of Lemma [D.4](#A4.Thmtheorem4 "Lemma D.4. ‣ D.2.1 Bounding ∑_{𝑖=1}^𝐻∑_{(𝑠,𝑏)∈𝒮×ℬ}{𝑑_𝑖^{𝗉,𝜇^𝖽,𝜈^⋆}⁢(𝑠,𝜇^𝖽⁢(𝑠),𝑏)⁢𝐵_1} ‣ D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

First, we introduce auxiliary values and reward functions to control
$`\sum_{i=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b}^{0}}\big({\widehat{V}}\big)`$
as below: for any time step $`i`$

- •
  $`\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}\coloneqq\min_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{V}_{i}^{+}(s)`$:
  the minimum value of all the entries in vector
  $`\widehat{V}_{i}^{+}`$.
- •
  $`\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\coloneqq\widehat{V}_{i}^{+}-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}1`$:
  truncated value function.
- •
  $`\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}(s)=\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\widehat{r}_{i}(s,a,b)`$:
  average reward function.
- •
  $`\widehat{r}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}=r_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}+\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\mathsf{m}}-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}\right)1`$:
  truncated reward function.

Applying the robust Bellman’s consistency equation in
([30](#A2.E30 "In B.2 Facts of RTZMGs and empirical RTZMGs ‣ Appendix B Preliminaries ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}=\widehat{V}_{i}^{+}-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}1`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(i)}}{\leq}\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}+\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}+2\beta_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}1`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}+\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}+\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\mathsf{m}}1-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}1\right)-\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\mathsf{m}}1+2\beta_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{r}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}+\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}-\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\mathsf{m}}1+2\beta_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\widehat{r}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}+\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}+2\beta_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}},`$ |  | (196) |

where (i) follows from the fact that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{i}^{+}(s)\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}(s)+\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big(\widehat{P}_{i,s,a,b}^{0}\big)}P\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}+\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}(s)+\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\Bigg[\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big({P}_{i,s,a,b}^{0}\big)}P\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left|\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big(\widehat{P}_{i,s,a,b}^{0}\big)}P\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}-\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big({P}_{i,s,a,b}^{0}\big)}P\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}\right|\Bigg]+\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}(s)+\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\left[{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}\right]+2\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{i}^{{\mu}^{\sf{d}},{\nu}^{\star}}(s)+\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}+2\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s),`$ |  | (197) |

\(ii\) is valid under the notation

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\coloneqq\mathrm{argmin}_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big(P^{0}_{i,s,a,b}\big)}{P}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+},`$ |  | (198) |

and (iii) holds under the notation as
$`\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}`$
and the sequence as
$`\widetilde{P}^{\inf,V}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{S\times S}`$. Besides, (i) in
([197](#A7.Ex90 "In G.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
exists due to
([48](#A4.E48 "In D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
in
Lemma [D.1](#A4.Thmtheorem1 "Lemma D.1. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
for $`N_{i}(s,a,b)>0`$ and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\left({P}^{0}_{i,s,a,b}\right)}\!P\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}-\inf_{P\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}\big(\widehat{P}_{i,s,a,b}^{0}\big)}P\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}\right|\leq H=\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\text{ for }N_{i}(s,a,b)=0.`$ |  | (199) |

Then, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\left[P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\big(P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\circ\big(P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\left[P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\big({P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\circ\big({P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\circ\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)+\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)-\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\circ\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)+\left(\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)-\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right)\circ\left(\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)+\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)+\left(\widehat{r}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}(s)+2\beta_{h}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\right)\circ\left(\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)+\big(\widetilde{P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iv)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)+\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\left(2\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)+1\right),`$ |  | (200) |

where (i) follows from that
$`\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i,s}^{\inf,V}}(V-b1)=\mathrm{Var}_{P_{i,s}^{\inf,V}}(V)`$
for any value vector $`V\in\mathbb{R}^{S}`$ and scalar $`b`$, (ii) holds
since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\left[\big({P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\circ\big({P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right]`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\left[\big({P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right]\circ\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\left[\big({P}^{\inf,\widehat{V}}_{i,s,a,b}\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\big)\right],`$ |  |

\(iii\) comes from
([196](#A7.Ex87 "In G.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix G Proofs of lemmas for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
and (iv) arises from $`\widehat{r}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}\leq r_{i}\leq 1`$
due to
$`\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\mathsf{m}}-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\mathsf{m}}\leq 0`$ by
definition.

Consequently, combining
([62](#A4.E62 "In D.2 Step 2: Decomposing the error Gap(𝜇̂,𝜈̂) ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we arrive at

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\mathbb{E}_{(a,b)\sim(\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),\nu^{\star}(s))}\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\Big(\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\left(2\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)+1\right)\Big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\left(\widetilde{P}_{i,s}^{\inf,V}\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\circ\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right)-\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\right)+\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\left(d_{i+1}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}(s)\!\circ\!\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}(s)\right)\!-\!d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\right)+\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\left(d_{i+1}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\left(\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}(s)\!\circ\!\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}(s)\right)\!-\!d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}(s)\right)+\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\beta_{i}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b,\widehat{V}).`$ |  | (201) |

Besides, under TV distance, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{0}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)-\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{0}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}}\big)-\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left\|P_{i,s,a,b}^{0}-P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}\right\|_{1}\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sigma^{+}\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}^{2}\leq(H+1)\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty},`$ |  | (202) |

where the last inequality comes from
Lemma [D.3](#A4.Thmtheorem3 "Lemma D.3. ‣ D.1 Step 1: Decoupling statistical dependency ‣ Appendix D Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning").

Therefore, we derive

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{0}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\left|\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{0}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)-\mathsf{Var}_{P_{i,s,a,b}^{\inf,V}}\big({\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{+}}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{H}2\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\beta_{i}^{\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)+\sum_{i=1}^{H}\left(\left\|\widehat{V}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}+(H+2)\left\|\widehat{V}_{i+1}^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}d_{H+1}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s)\widehat{V}_{H+1}^{\prime}(s)\circ\widehat{V}_{H+1}^{\prime}(s)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle 4\sum_{i=1}^{H}\min\left\{\frac{\left(H+1\right)\left(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H-i}\right)}{\sigma^{+}},H\right\}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\beta_{i}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b,\widehat{V})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+(H+3)\sum_{i=1}^{H}\min\left\{\frac{\left(H+1\right)\left(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H-i}\right)}{\sigma^{+}},H\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(i)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle 4\sum_{i=1}^{H}\min\left\{\frac{\left(H+1\right)\left(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H-i}\right)}{\sigma^{+}},H\right\}\sum_{i=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\beta_{i}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b,\widehat{V})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+(H+3)\sum_{i=1}^{H}\min\left\{\frac{\left(H+1\right)\left(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H-i}\right)}{\sigma^{+}},H\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(ii)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle 4H\min\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma^{+}-1+(1-\sigma^{+})^{H})}{(\sigma^{+})^{2}},H\right\}\sum_{i=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{B}}d_{i}^{\sf{p},\mu^{\sf{d}},\nu^{\star}}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b)\beta_{i}(s,\mu^{\sf{d}}(s),b,\widehat{V})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+(H+3)H\min\left\{\frac{2(H\sigma^{+}-1+(1-\sigma^{+})^{H})}{(\sigma^{+})^{2}},H\right\},`$ |  | (203) |

where (i) comes from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the (ii) holds since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{H}\frac{\left(H+1\right)\left(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H-i}\right)}{\sigma^{+}}=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{H(H+1)}{\sigma^{+}}-\sum_{i=0}^{H-1}\frac{(H+1)(1-\sigma^{+})^{i}}{\sigma^{+}}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{H(H+1)}{\sigma^{+}}-\frac{(H+1)(1-(1-\sigma^{+})^{H})}{(\sigma^{+})^{2}}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{(H+1)(H\sigma^{+}-1+(1-\sigma^{+})^{H})}{(\sigma^{+})^{2}}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{2H(H\sigma^{+}-1+(1-\sigma^{+})^{H})}{(\sigma^{+})^{2}}.`$ |  |

## Appendix H Proof for Theorem [4.4](#S4.Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4.4 (Lower bound for RTZMGs). ‣ Robust unilateral clipped concentrability. ‣ 4 Performance Guarantees ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

To establish
Theorem [4.4](#S4.Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4.4 (Lower bound for RTZMGs). ‣ Robust unilateral clipped concentrability. ‣ 4 Performance Guarantees ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"),
we present key auxiliary facts, each addressing critical components of
the proof and providing essential theoretical underpinnings.

### H.1 Proof of Lemma [E.2](#A5.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")

Since all RMDPs in $`\mathcal{M}(\mathcal{F},\Phi)`$ are constructed
similarly for each $`w\in\mathcal{F}`$ and $`\phi\in\Phi`$, we will
focus on a specific RMDP
$`\mathcal{M}_{f}^{\phi}\in\mathcal{M}(\mathcal{F},\Phi)`$, with the
results applicable to all other RMDPs in
$`\mathcal{M}(\mathcal{F},\Phi)`$.

#### H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states

Before proceeding, we introduce several facts and notations that will be
useful throughout this section. First, for any
$`\mathcal{M}_{f}^{\phi}`$ and any policy $`\widetilde{\mu}`$, we
observe the following at the final step $`H+1`$:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall s\in\mathcal{M}\cup\mathcal{N}:\quad V_{H+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)=0.`$ |  | (204) |

Then for step $`H`$, we can easily verify that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall s\in\mathcal{N}:\quad V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\widetilde{\mu}_{H}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[r_{H}(s,a)+\inf_{\mathcal{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{f,\phi}_{H,s,a})}\mathcal{P}V_{H+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}\right]=1`$ |  | (205a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall s\in\mathcal{M}:\quad V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\widetilde{\mu}_{H}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[r_{H}(s,a)+\inf_{\mathcal{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{f,\phi}_{H,s,a})}\mathcal{P}V_{H+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}\right]=0,`$ |  | (205b) |

which holds by
([204](#A8.E204 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and the definition of the reward function (see
([E.1.1](#A5.EGx89 "E.1.1 Constructing hard robust MDP instances ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))).
The above fact directly indicates that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(s,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad\min_{\widetilde{s}\in\mathcal{S}}V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(\widetilde{s})=V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})\leq V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)<V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime}),`$ |  | (206a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(s,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{N}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)=V_{H}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime}).`$ |  | (206b) |

Then we introduce a claim which we will prove by induction in a moment
as below:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(h,s,s^{\prime})\in[H]\times\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})\leq V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)<V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime})`$ |  | (207a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(s,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{N}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)=V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime}).`$ |  | (207b) |

Note that the base case when the time step is $`H+1`$ is verified in
([206](#A8.E206 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
Assume that the following fact at time step $`h+1`$ holds

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(s,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad\min_{\widetilde{s}\in\mathcal{S}}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(\widetilde{s})=V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})\leq V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)<V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime}),`$ |  | (208a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(s,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{N}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)=V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime}).`$ |  | (208b) |

The rest of the proof focuses on proving the same property for time step
$`h`$. For RMDP
$`\mathcal{M}_{f}^{\phi}\in\mathcal{M}(\mathcal{F},\Phi)`$ and any
policy $`\widetilde{\mu}`$, we characterize the robust value function of
different states separately:

- •
  For state $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$: we observe that for any
  $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$,

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\bigg[r_{h}(s,a)+\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{f,\phi}_{h,s,a})}{P}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}\bigg]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}1+\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,s)}\left[{P}_{h}^{\inf,f,\phi}(s\,|\,s,a)V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)\right]+\sigma^{+}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=1+(1-\sigma^{+})V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)+\sigma^{+}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f}),`$ |  | (209) |

  where (i) holds by $`r_{h}(s,a)=1`$ for all $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$ (see
  ([E.1.1](#A5.EGx89 "E.1.1 Constructing hard robust MDP instances ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))),
  the fact that
  $`\min_{\widetilde{s}\in\mathcal{S}}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(\widetilde{s})=V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$
  induced by the induction assumption
  (cf. ([208](#A8.E208 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")))
  and the definition of $`{P}_{h}^{\inf,f,\phi}(s\,|\,s,a)`$ in
  ([93](#A5.E93 "In E.1.2 Uncertainty set of the transition kernels ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  and the last equality follows from $`P^{f,\phi}(s\,|\,s,a)=1`$ for all
  $`(s,a)\in\mathcal{N}\times\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{one}}`$. Resorting to
  the induction assumption in
  ([208](#A8.E208 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  we have

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\forall(s,s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{N}\times\mathcal{N}:`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s)=V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(s^{\prime}).`$ |  | (210) |
- •
  For state $`m_{f}`$: first, the robust value function at state
  $`m_{f}`$ obeys

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{a\sim\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})}\left[r_{h}(m_{f},a)+\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{f,\phi}_{h,m_{f},a})}{P}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}\right]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}0+\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{f,\phi}_{h,m_{f},\phi_{h}})}{P}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(1-\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\inf_{{P}\in\mathcal{U}^{\sigma^{+}}(P^{f,\phi}_{h,m_{f},1-\phi_{h}})}{P}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{=}\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\Big[{p}^{\inf}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+\left(1-{p}^{\inf}\right)V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})\Big]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(1-\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\Big[{q}^{\inf}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+\left(1-{q}^{\inf}\right)V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})\Big]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{=}m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+(1-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi})V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  | (211) |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq(1-\sigma^{+})V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+\sigma^{+}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f}).`$ |  | (212) |

  where (i) uses the definition of the robust value function and the
  reward function in
  ([E.1.1](#A5.EGx89 "E.1.1 Constructing hard robust MDP instances ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")), (ii)
  uses the induction assumption in
  ([208](#A8.E208 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  so that the minimum is attained by picking the choice specified in
  ([94](#A5.Ex88 "In E.1.2 Uncertainty set of the transition kernels ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  to absorb probability mass to state $`m_{f}`$, and (iii) holds by
  plugging in the definition
  ([96](#A5.E96 "In Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  of $`m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}`$. Finally, the last inequality
  follows from the fact that function
  $`f(m)\coloneqq mV_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+(1-m)V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$
  is monotonically increasing with $`m`$ since
  $`V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})>V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$
  (see the induction assumption
  ([208](#A8.E208 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))),
  and the fact $`m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\leq 1-\sigma^{+}`$.

Combining the above results with
([210](#A8.E210 "In 1st item ‣ H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we confirm the claim in
([207](#A8.E207 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

#### H.1.2 Deriving the optimal policy and optimal robust value function

We shall characterize the optimal policy and corresponding optimal
robust value function for different states separately:

- •
  For states in $`\mathcal{M}`$: Recall
  ([211](#A8.E211 "In 2nd item ‣ H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ | $`\displaystyle=m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+(1-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi})V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  | (213) |

  and the fact
  $`V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})>V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$
  in
  ([207](#A8.E207 "In H.1.1 Ordering the robust value function over different states ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
  We observe that
  ([213](#A8.E213 "In 1st item ‣ H.1.2 Deriving the optimal policy and optimal robust value function ‣ H.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  is monotonicity increasing w.r.t. $`m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}`$,
  and $`m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}`$ is also increasing in
  $`\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})`$ (refer to the fact
  $`{p}^{\inf}\geq{q}^{\inf}`$ since $`p\geq q`$; see
  ([104](#A5.E104 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  and
  ([94](#A5.Ex88 "In E.1.2 Uncertainty set of the transition kernels ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))).
  Consequently, the optimal policy and optimal robust value function in
  state $`m_{f}`$ thus obey

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\forall h\in[H]:\quad`$ | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})=1,`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f})={p}^{\inf}V_{h+1}^{\star,\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(n_{f})+\left(1-{p}^{\inf}\right)V_{h+1}^{\star,\sigma^{+},f,\phi}(m_{f}).`$ |  | (214) |
- •
  For states $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$: Recall the transitions in
  ([E.1.4](#A5.EGx100 "E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  and
  ([E.1.1](#A5.EGx88 "E.1.1 Constructing hard robust MDP instances ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
  Considering that the action does not influence the state transition
  for all states $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$, without loss of generality, we
  choose the robust optimal policy obeying

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\forall s\in\mathcal{N}:\quad\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\phi_{h}\,|\,s)=1.`$ |  | (215) |
````
