````math
### H.2 Proof of ([110](#A5.E110 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))

With the initial state distribution and behavior policy defined in
([100](#A5.E100 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we have for any MDP $`\mathcal{M}_{\phi}^{f}`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(s)=\varrho^{\mathsf{n}}(s)=\overline{\varrho}(s),`$ |  |

which leads to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall(m_{f},a)\in\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{one}}:\quad d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(m_{f},a)=\frac{1}{2}\overline{\varrho}(m_{f}).`$ |  | (216) |

Along with
$`d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(n_{f},a)=\frac{1}{2}\overline{\varrho}(n_{f})=0`$, ([110a](#A5.E110.1 "In 110 ‣ E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
is proved.

In view of
([E.1.4](#A5.EGx100 "E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
the state occupancy distribution at any step $`h=2,3,\cdots,H`$ obeys

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(m_{f})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{P}\left\{s_{h}=s^{\prime}\,|\,s_{h-1}=m_{f};\widetilde{\mu}^{\mathsf{n}}\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h-1}(m_{f})\left[\widetilde{\mu}^{\mathsf{n}}_{h-1}(\phi_{h-1}\,|\,m_{f})(1-p-\Delta)+\widetilde{\mu}^{\mathsf{n}}_{h-1}(1-\phi_{h-1}\,|\,m_{f})(1-p)\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h-1}(m_{f})(1-p-\Delta)\geq\cdots\geq d_{1}^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi}}(m_{f})\prod_{j=0}^{h-1}(1-p-\Delta)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq d_{1}^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi}}(m_{f})\Big(1-p-\Delta\Big)^{H}>\frac{\overline{\varrho}(m_{f})}{2},`$ |  | (217) |

where the last line makes use of the properties $`p`$ and $`\Delta`$ in
([106](#A5.E106 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Big(1-p-\Delta\Big)^{H}\geq\Big(1-\frac{c_{2}}{2H}\Big)^{H}\geq\Big(1-\frac{1}{2H}\Big)^{H}\geq\frac{1}{2},`$ |  |

provided that $`0<c_{2}<1`$. In addition, as state $`n_{f}`$ is an
absorbing state and state $`m_{f}`$ will only transfer to itself or
state $`n_{f}`$ at each time step, we directly achieve that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(m_{f})\leq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h-1}(m_{f})\leq\cdots\leq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(m_{f})\leq\overline{\varrho}(m_{f}).`$ |  | (218) |

For state $`n_{f}`$, as it is absorbing, we directly have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(n_{f})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left\{s_{h}=n_{f}\,|\,s_{h-1}=n_{f};\widetilde{\mu}^{\mathsf{n}}\right\}\geq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h-1}(n_{f})\geq\cdots\geq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(n_{f})=\overline{\varrho}(n_{f}).`$ |  | (219) |

According to the assumption in
([109](#A5.E109 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
it is easily verified that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(n_{f})\leq 1\leq 2\overline{\varrho}(n_{f}).`$ |  | (220) |

Finally, combining
([217](#A8.E217 "In H.2 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
([218](#A8.E218 "In H.2 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
([219](#A8.E219 "In H.2 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
([220](#A8.E220 "In H.2 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
the definitions of $`P_{h}^{\star}(\cdot\,|\,s,a)`$ in
([E.1.4](#A5.EGx100 "E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
and the Markov property, we arrive at for any
$`(h,s)\in[H]\times\mathcal{S}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\varrho}(s)}{2}\leq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s)\leq 2\overline{\varrho}(s),`$ |  | (221) |

which directly leads to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\varrho}(s)}{4}\leq d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s,a)=\widetilde{\mu}^{\mathsf{n}}_{1}(a\,|\,s)d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s)\leq\overline{\varrho}(s).`$ |  | (222) |

### H.3 Proof of ([112](#A5.E112 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))

Examining the definition of $`C^{\star}_{\mathrm{r}}`$ in
([20](#S4.Ex4 "In Definition 4.1 (Robust unilateral clipped concentrability). ‣ Robust unilateral clipped concentrability. ‣ 4 Performance Guarantees ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we make the following observations.

- •
  For $`h=1`$, we have

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\max_{(s,a,P)\in\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{one}}\times\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{one}}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{\min\big\{d_{1}^{\star,P}(s,a),\frac{1}{4SA}\big\}}{d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(s,a)}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{(s,P)\in\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{\min\big\{d_{1}^{\star,P}(s,\phi_{1}),\frac{1}{4SA}\big\}}{d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(s,\phi_{1})}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{(s,P)\in\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{1}{4SAd^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{1}(s,\phi_{1})}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{s\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{1}{2SA\overline{\varrho}(s)}=C,`$ |  | (223) |

  where (i) holds by $`d_{1}^{\star,P}(s)=\rho(s)=0`$ for all
  $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$ (see
  ([111](#A5.E111 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")))
  and $`\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,\phi}(\phi_{h}\,|\,s)=1`$ for all
  $`(s,h)\in\mathcal{M}\times[H]`$ (see
  ([98](#A5.E98 "In Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))), (ii)
  follows from the fact that $`d_{1}^{\star,P}(s,\phi_{1})=1`$ for all
  $`s\in\mathcal{M}`$, (iii) is verified in
  ([110](#A5.E110 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  and the last equality arises from the definition in
  ([108](#A5.E108 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
- •
  Similarly, for $`h=2,3,\cdots,H`$, we arrive at

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\max_{(s,a,P)\in\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{one}}\times\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{one}}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{\min\big\{d_{h}^{\star,P}(s,a),\frac{1}{4SA}\big\}}{d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s,a)}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{(s,P)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{\min\big\{d_{h}^{\star,P}(s,\phi_{h}),\frac{1}{4SA}\big\}}{d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s,\phi_{h})}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{(s,P)\in\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{1}{4SAd^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s,\phi_{h})}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\max_{s\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{1}{2SA\overline{\varrho}(s)}=2C,`$ |  | (224) |

  where (i) holds by the optimal policy in
  ([98](#A5.E98 "In Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  and the trivial fact that $`d_{h}^{\star,P}(s)=0`$ for all
  $`s\in\mathcal{N}`$ (see
  ([111](#A5.E111 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  and
  ([E.1.4](#A5.EGx100 "E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))), (ii)
  arises from
  ([110](#A5.E110 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  and the last equality comes from
  ([108](#A5.E108 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

Combining the above cases, we complete the proof by

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{C}{2}\leq C^{\star}_{\mathrm{r}}=\max_{(h,s,a,P)\in[H]\times\mathcal{S}_{\mathsf{one}}\times\mathcal{A}_{\mathsf{one}}\times\mathcal{U}^{\sigma}(P^{\phi})}\frac{\min\big\{d_{h}^{\star,P}(s,a),\frac{1}{4SA}\big\}}{d^{\mathsf{n},P^{\phi,f}}_{h}(s,a)}\leq C.`$ |  |

### H.4 Proof of ([117](#A5.E117 "In E.2.1 Step 1: Converting the goal to estimate (𝑓,ϕ) ‣ E.2 Step 2: Establishing the minimax lower bound ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))

Recalling
([97a](#A5.E97.1 "In 97 ‣ Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([99](#A5.E99 "In Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we first consider a more general form

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle{p}^{\inf}V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})+(1-{p}^{\inf})V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\left(m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})+\left[1-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\right]V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left({p}^{\inf}-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\right)V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})+m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+(1-{p}^{\inf})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)-\left({p}^{\inf}-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\right)V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})\right)+(1-{p}^{\inf})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left({p}^{\inf}-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle(1-{p}^{\inf})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\left({p}^{\inf}-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle(1-{p}^{\inf})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(p-q)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right),`$ |  | (225) |

where the last inequality holds since

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle{p}^{\inf}-m_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},f,\phi}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\big({p}^{\inf}-{q}^{\inf}\big)\big(1-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=(p-q)\big(1-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(p-q)\big(1-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})+\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(1-\phi_{h}\,|\,m_{f})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(p-q)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1},`$ |  | (226) |

with the first equality holding by
([96](#A5.E96 "In Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
and the second existing by
([94](#A5.Ex88 "In E.1.2 Uncertainty set of the transition kernels ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).

To further control
([225](#A8.Ex21 "In H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\overset{\text{(i)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle 1+(1-{\sigma^{+}})V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})+{\sigma^{+}}V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\left({p}^{\inf}V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})+(1-{p}^{\inf})V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle 1+(1-{p}^{\inf}-{\sigma^{+}})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\text{(ii)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle 1+(1-p)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\cdots=\sum_{j=0}^{H-h}(1-p)^{j},`$ |  | (227) |

where (i) follows from
Lemma [E.2](#A5.Thmtheorem2 "Lemma E.2. ‣ E.1.3 Robust value functions and optimal policies ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")
and (ii) holds by
([94](#A5.Ex88 "In E.1.2 Uncertainty set of the transition kernels ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
Then, we consider two cases w.r.t. the uncertainty level
$`{\sigma^{+}}`$ to control
([227](#A8.Ex34 "In H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
respectively:

- •
  When $`0<{\sigma^{+}}\leq\frac{c_{2}}{2H}`$: Recall
  $`p=\frac{c_{2}}{H}`$ if $`{\sigma^{+}}\leq\frac{c_{2}}{2H}`$. In this
  case, applying
  ([227](#A8.Ex34 "In H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  we have

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{H-h}(1-p)^{j}\geq\sum_{j=0}^{H-h}\left(1-\frac{c_{2}}{H}\right)^{j}=\frac{1-\left(1-\frac{c_{2}}{H}\right)^{H-h+1}}{c_{2}/H}\geq\frac{2c_{2}(H-h+1)}{3}.`$ |  | (228) |

  Here, the final inequality holds by observing

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{c_{2}}{H}\right)^{H-h+1}\leq\exp\left(-\frac{c_{2}(H-h+1)}{H}\right)\leq 1-\frac{2c_{2}(H-h+1)}{3H},`$ |  | (229) |

  where the first inequality holds by noticing $`c_{2}<\frac{1}{2}`$ and
  then $`1-x\leq\exp(-x)`$, and the last inequality holds by
  $`\exp(-x)\leq 1-\frac{2x}{3}`$ for any $`0\leq x\leq\frac{1}{2}`$.

  Plugging above fact in
  ([228](#A8.Ex38 "In 1st item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  back to
  ([225](#A8.Ex21 "In H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  we arrive at

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle(1-{p}^{\inf})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(p-q)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}\frac{2c_{2}(H-h+1)}{3}.`$ |  | (230) |

  Then, invoking the assumption

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}\geq\frac{H}{8}`$ |  | (231) |

  in
  ([116](#A5.E116 "In E.2.1 Step 1: Converting the goal to estimate (𝑓,ϕ) ‣ E.2 Step 2: Establishing the minimax lower bound ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  and applying
  ([230](#A8.Ex39 "In 1st item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  recursively for $`h=1,2,\cdots,H`$ yields

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{3}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(1-{p}^{\inf})^{h-1}(p-q)(H-h+1)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\geq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{3}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(1-\frac{c_{2}}{H})^{h-1}(p-q)(H-h+1)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\geq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{6}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(p-q)(H-h+1)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}\Delta}{6}\sum_{h=1}^{H}h\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{H-h+1}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{H-h+1}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iv)}}{\geq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}\Delta}{6}\sum_{h=1}^{\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor}2h\geq\frac{c_{2}\Delta}{6}\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor\left(\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor+1\right),`$ |  | (232) |

  where (i) follows from $`1-{p}^{\inf}\geq 1-p=1-\frac{c_{2}}{H}`$,
  and (ii) holds by

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\forall h\in[H]:\quad(1-\frac{c_{2}}{H})^{h-1}\geq(1-\frac{c_{2}}{H})^{H}\geq\frac{1}{2}b`$ |  | (233) |

  as long as $`c_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}`$. Here, (iii) arises from the
  definition of $`p,q`$ in
  ([104](#A5.E104 "In E.1.4 Construction of the history/batch dataset ‣ E.1 Step 1: Constructing a family of hard Markov game instances ‣ Appendix E Proof of Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")); (iv)
  can be verified by the fact that for any series
  $`0\leq m_{1},m_{2},\cdots,m_{H}\leq m_{\max}`$ that obeys
  $`\sum_{h=1}^{H}m_{h}\geq y`$, one has

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}m_{h}h\geq\sum_{h=1}^{\left\lfloor m_{\max}/n\right\rfloor}m_{\max}h,`$ |  | (234) |

  and taking
  $`m_{h}=\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{H-h+1}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{H-h+1}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}\leq 2=m_{\max}`$
  and $`n=\frac{H}{8}`$.

  Consequently, observed from
  ([232](#A8.Ex41 "In 1st item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  the following inequality holds

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}\Delta}{6}\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor\left(\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor+1\right)\geq c_{3}\Delta H^{2}>\varepsilon`$ |  | (235) |

  for some small enough constant $`c_{3}`$ and letting
  $`\Delta=\frac{\varepsilon}{c_{3}H^{2}}`$.
- •
  When $`\frac{c_{2}}{2H}<{\sigma^{+}}\leq 1-c_{0}`$: Similarly,
  recalling $`p=\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right)\sigma^{+}`$ if
  $`{\sigma^{+}}>\frac{c_{2}}{2H}`$ and invoking
  ([227](#A8.Ex34 "In H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  gives

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(n_{f})-V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{H-h}(1-p)^{j}=\sum_{j=0}^{H-h}\left(1-\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right){\sigma^{+}}\right)^{j}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{1-\left(1-(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}){\sigma^{+}}\right)^{H-h+1}}{(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}){\sigma^{+}}}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}(H-h+1)}{3{\sigma^{+}}H},`$ |  | (236) |

  where the final inequality holds by observing

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\left(1-\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right){\sigma^{+}}\right)^{H-h+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right){\sigma^{+}}(H-h+1)\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}\exp\left(-\frac{c_{2}}{2H}\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right)(H-h+1)\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 1-\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right)\frac{c_{2}(H-h+1)}{3H}.`$ |  | (237) |

  Here, (i) holds by observing $`\frac{c_{2}}{2H}<{\sigma^{+}}`$, and
  the last inequality holds by $`\left(1+\frac{c_{1}}{H}\right)\leq 2`$,
  $`c_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}`$, and the fact $`\exp(-x)\leq 1-\frac{2x}{3}`$
  for any $`0\leq x\leq\frac{1}{2}`$.

  Plugging
  ([236](#A8.Ex46 "In 2nd item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  into
  ([225](#A8.Ex21 "In H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  gives

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle(1-{p}^{\inf})\left(V_{h+1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{h+1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(p-q)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}\frac{c_{2}(H-h+1)}{3{\sigma^{+}}H}.`$ |  | (238) |

  Following the steps to achieve
  ([232](#A8.Ex41 "In 1st item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")),
  applying
  ([238](#A8.Ex50 "In 2nd item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  recursively for $`h=1,2,\cdots,H`$ gives

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}(1-{p}^{\inf})^{h-1}(p-q)\frac{c_{2}(H-h+1)}{6{\sigma^{+}}H}\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}(p-q)}{6{\sigma^{+}}H}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(1-\frac{c_{1}}{H})^{h-1}(H-h+1)\big\|\widetilde{\mu}_{h}^{\star,f,\phi}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})-\widetilde{\mu}_{h}(\cdot\,|\,m_{f})\big\|_{1}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\geq}`$ | $`\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}\Delta}{12{\sigma^{+}}H}\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor\left(\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor+1\right),`$ |  | (239) |

  where (i) follows from
  $`1-{p}^{\inf}=1-(p-{\sigma^{+}})=1-\frac{c_{1}}{H}{\sigma^{+}}`$,
  and (ii) holds by letting $`c_{1}\leq\frac{1}{2}`$ and following the
  same routine of
  ([232](#A8.Ex41 "In 1st item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning")).
  Consequently,
  ([239](#A8.Ex52 "In 2nd item ‣ H.4 Proof of () ‣ Appendix H Proof for Theorem ‣ Sample-Efficient Tabular Self-Play forOffline Robust Reinforcement Learning"))
  yields

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}^{\star,{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})-V_{1}^{\widetilde{\mu},{\sigma^{+}},f,\phi}(m_{f})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}\Delta}{12{\sigma^{+}}H}\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor\left(\left\lfloor H/16\right\rfloor+1\right)\geq\frac{c_{4}\Delta H}{{\sigma^{+}}}>\varepsilon,`$ |  | (240) |

  which holds for some small enough constant $`c_{4}`$ and letting
  $`\Delta=\frac{{\sigma^{+}}\varepsilon}{c_{4}H}`$.
````
